const searchVerifyUrl = "https:\/\/www.soft112.com\/api\/search-verify";
const searchRecaptchaSiteKey = "6LdsT4MkAAAAAHHqan-X0Wb5eIxSpgMhDbheMCU0";
const searchRecaptchaSrc = "https:\/\/www.google.com\/recaptcha\/api.js?hl=en\u0026render=explicit";
const searchCaptchaInvalidMessage = "Captcha invalid !";
const searchTermUrl = "https:\/\/search.soft112.com\/##term##.html";
let searchRecaptchaWidget = null;
let searchRecaptchaLoading = null;
let searchPendingUrl = null;
let searchCaptchaBusy = false;
function markSearchCaptchaFailed(message) {
searchCaptchaBusy = false;
const gate = document.getElementById('search-captcha-gate');
if (gate && message) {
gate.textContent = message;
}
if (message && typeof notyf !== 'undefined') {
notyf.error(message);
}
}
function loadSearchRecaptcha() {
if (window.grecaptcha && typeof window.grecaptcha.render === 'function') {
return Promise.resolve();
}
if (searchRecaptchaLoading) {
return searchRecaptchaLoading;
}
searchRecaptchaLoading = new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
window.onSearchRecaptchaLoad = function () {
resolve();
};
const script = document.createElement('script');
script.src = searchRecaptchaSrc + '&onload=onSearchRecaptchaLoad';
script.async = true;
script.onerror = function () {
searchRecaptchaLoading = null;
reject();
};
document.head.appendChild(script);
});
return searchRecaptchaLoading;
}
function ensureSearchRecaptchaWidget() {
if (searchRecaptchaWidget !== null) {
return searchRecaptchaWidget;
}
searchRecaptchaWidget = grecaptcha.render('search-recaptcha', {
sitekey: searchRecaptchaSiteKey,
size: 'invisible',
badge: 'bottomright',
callback: onSearchRecaptchaToken,
'error-callback': function () {
markSearchCaptchaFailed(searchCaptchaInvalidMessage);
}
});
return searchRecaptchaWidget;
}
function onSearchRecaptchaToken(token) {
if (!token) {
markSearchCaptchaFailed(searchCaptchaInvalidMessage);
return;
}
axios({
method: 'POST',
url: searchVerifyUrl,
data: { 'g-recaptcha-response': token },
withCredentials: true,
headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Accept': 'application/json' }
}).then(function (response) {
if (!response.data || response.data.status !== 'OK') {
markSearchCaptchaFailed(searchCaptchaInvalidMessage);
return;
}
if (searchPendingUrl) {
self.location = searchPendingUrl;
return;
}
try {
sessionStorage.setItem('search-captcha-reloaded', '1');
} catch (e) {}
self.location.reload();
}).catch(function (error) {
let message = searchCaptchaInvalidMessage;
let toasted = false;
if (error.response) {
const errors = error.response.data.errors;
if (typeof errors !== 'undefined') {
for (const messages of Object.values(errors)) {
messages.forEach(function (item) {
message = item;
toasted = true;
if (typeof notyf !== 'undefined') {
notyf.error({ message: item, duration: 3500 });
}
});
}
} else if (error.response.data && error.response.data.message) {
message = error.response.data.message;
}
if (error.response.data && error.response.data['recaptcha-rev'] === true && window.grecaptcha && searchRecaptchaWidget !== null) {
grecaptcha.reset(searchRecaptchaWidget);
}
}
if (toasted) {
searchCaptchaBusy = false;
const gate = document.getElementById('search-captcha-gate');
if (gate && message) {
gate.textContent = message;
}
return;
}
markSearchCaptchaFailed(message);
});
}
function runSearchRecaptcha() {
loadSearchRecaptcha().then(function () {
grecaptcha.ready(function () {
grecaptcha.execute(ensureSearchRecaptchaWidget());
});
}).catch(function () {
markSearchCaptchaFailed(searchCaptchaInvalidMessage);
});
}
function searchVerifyThenReload() {
searchPendingUrl = null;
let alreadyReloaded = false;
try {
alreadyReloaded = sessionStorage.getItem('search-captcha-reloaded') === '1';
if (alreadyReloaded) {
sessionStorage.removeItem('search-captcha-reloaded');
}
} catch (e) {}
if (alreadyReloaded) {
const next = new URL(window.location.href);
if (next.searchParams.has('nc')) {
markSearchCaptchaFailed(searchCaptchaInvalidMessage);
return;
}
next.searchParams.set('nc', String(Date.now()));
window.location.replace(next.toString());
return;
}
searchCaptchaBusy = true;
runSearchRecaptcha();
}
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
if (document.getElementById('search-captcha-gate') || !window.sessionStorage) {
return;
}

sessionStorage.removeItem('search-captcha-reloaded');
} catch (e) {}
});
const searchTerm = (id) => {

self.location = destination;
return false;
}

return false;
}

searchCaptchaBusy = true;
runSearchRecaptcha();
return false;

// Navigator clipboard api needs a secure context (https)
if (navigator.clipboard && window.isSecureContext) {
await navigator.clipboard.writeText(textToCopy);
} else {
// Use the 'out of viewport hidden text area' trick
const textArea = document.createElement("textarea");
textArea.value = textToCopy;
// Move textarea out of the viewport so it's not visible
textArea.style.position = "absolute";
textArea.style.left = "-999999px";
document.body.prepend(textArea);
textArea.select();
try {
document.execCommand('copy');
} catch (error) {
console.error(error);
} finally {
textArea.remove();
}
}
